- **prescreen-benchmark**: Benchmark local LLMs (Ollama models such as qwen3:14b, hermes3:8b) as CiteVahti citation prescreening agents on a chosen topic, and build the publication-style prescreen report + a dedicated CiteVahti store from the run. Use this whenever the user wants to test how well small/local models rate claim-vs-source support, add a new topic/theme to the prescreen study, compare models against Claude or against a guideline anchor, compute agreement / Cohen's kappa, produce a shareable results page "to show on the website", grow the de-identified validation corpus, or investigate where a model's ratin… ([heidihelena/citevahti](https://github.com/heidihelena/citevahti/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/prescreen-benchmark) · ★1)
- **prescreen-calibration**: 주제 사전 점검표(topic-selection)가 너무 엄하거나 느슨하지 않은지, 본회의 가능성을 실제로 가르는지 이미 채택된 논문으로 확인하고 고치는 절차. 목표 학회의 본회의·Findings(가능하면 미채택) 논문을 결과를 보기 전에 정한 규칙으로 뽑아 점검표에 넣고, 어느 항목에서 몇 편이 떨어지는지 센다. 후보가 연달아 전부 탈락할 때, 점검표 문구를 바꿨을 때, 새 학회를 노릴 때 쓴다. - **prescribe**: Ask the deck doctor one question about a deck — "I keep getting wrathed on five", "make it faster", "should I run Sol Ring" — and get a ranked, validated, skeptic-reviewed prescription (cuts priced, adds that close a named axis, up to ten). Reads the captain's log. Use when the pilot wants advice in response to a prompt or a pattern in their games, rather than the full diagnosis.
